Function Description:\+ Handles the case where a cgroup\textquotesingle{}s memory usage is over its high limit. It checks if current has over\+\_\+high pages and calls the internal handler. This triggers reclaim or other actions to bring usage back below the limit.

consume\+\_\+stock:\+ Try to consume stocked charge on this cpu. @memcg:\+ memcg to consume from. @nr\+\_\+pages:\+ how many pages to charge.

Consume the cached charge if enough nr\+\_\+pages are present otherwise return failure. Also return failure for charge request larger than MEMCG\+\_\+\+CHARGE\+\_\+\+BATCH or if the local lock is already taken.

returns true if successful, false otherwise.

get\+\_\+mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+from\+\_\+mm:\+ Obtain a reference on given mm\+\_\+struct\textquotesingle{}s memcg. @mm:\+ mm from which memcg should be extracted. It can be NULL.

Obtain a reference on mm-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}memcg and returns it if successful. If mm is NULL, then the memcg is chosen as follows:\+ 1) The active memcg, if set. 2) current-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}mm-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}memcg, if available 3) root memcg If \doxylink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup} is disabled, NULL is returned.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mem\_cgroup\_charge\_hugetlb()}{mem\_cgroup\_charge\_hugetlb()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8c_a742c75e31c77d006173e30ccd508635f} 
int mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+charge\+\_\+hugetlb (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{folio}{, }\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+charge\+\_\+hugetlb -\/ charge the memcg for a hugetlb folio @folio:\+ folio being charged @gfp:\+ reclaim mode

This function is called when allocating a huge page folio, after the page has already been obtained and charged to the appropriate hugetlb cgroup controller (if it is enabled).

Returns ENOMEM if the memcg is already full. Returns 0 if either the charge was successful, or if we skip the charging.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mem\_cgroup\_css\_from\_folio()}{mem\_cgroup\_css\_from\_folio()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8c_a5287cc35586457806d958c58996fb81d} 
struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structcgroup__subsys__state}{cgroup\+\_\+subsys\+\_\+state}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+css\+\_\+from\+\_\+folio (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{folio}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+css\+\_\+from\+\_\+folio -\/ css of the memcg associated with a folio @folio:\+ folio of interest

If memcg is bound to the default hierarchy, css of the memcg associated with @folio is returned. The returned css remains associated with @folio until it is released.

If memcg is bound to a traditional hierarchy, the css of root\+\_\+mem\+\_\+cgroup is returned.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mem\_cgroup\_css\_reset()}{mem\_cgroup\_css\_reset()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8c_a7de0beac3da2fdff490f380d6d291f4a} 
void mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+css\+\_\+reset (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structcgroup__subsys__state}{cgroup\+\_\+subsys\+\_\+state}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{css}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+css\+\_\+reset -\/ reset the states of a \doxylink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup} @css:\+ the target css

Reset the states of the \doxylink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup} associated with @css. This is invoked when the userland requests disabling on the default hierarchy but the memcg is pinned through dependency. The memcg should stop applying policies and should revert to the vanilla state as it may be made visible again.

The current implementation only resets the essential configurations. This needs to be expanded to cover all the visible parts. 

Definition at line \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8c_source_l03987}{3987}} of file \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8c_source}{memcontrol.\+c}}.

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+get\+\_\+oom\+\_\+group -\/ get a memory cgroup to clean up after OOM @victim:\+ task to be killed by the OOM killer @oom\+\_\+domain:\+ memcg in case of memcg OOM, NULL in case of system-\/wide OOM

Returns a pointer to a memory cgroup, which has to be cleaned up by killing all belonging OOM-\/killable tasks.

Caller has to call \doxylink{memcontrol_8h_a62c9264078ac378e5a5c70d94bd52d32}{mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+put()} on the returned non-\/\+NULL memcg.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8c_a6d922937402b079cafb2d90616168b0c} 
struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+iter (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{root}{, }\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup}{mem\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{prev}{, }\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structmem__cgroup__reclaim__cookie}{mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+reclaim\+\_\+cookie}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{reclaim}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+iter -\/ iterate over memory cgroup hierarchy @root:\+ hierarchy root @prev:\+ previously returned memcg, NULL on first invocation @reclaim:\+ cookie for shared reclaim walks, NULL for full walks

Returns references to children of the hierarchy below @root, or @root itself, or NULL after a full round-\/trip.

Caller must pass the return value in @prev on subsequent invocations for reference counting, or use \doxylink{memcontrol_8c_a9d0260b0dca7b94839c41a75ce96e596}{mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+iter\+\_\+break()} to cancel a hierarchy walk before the round-\/trip is complete.

Reclaimers can specify a node in @reclaim to divide up the memcgs in the hierarchy among all concurrent reclaimers operating on the same node.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8c_a3da1ba73ef841faed60e57d883bdbf54} 
void mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+migrate (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{old}{, }\item[{struct folio \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{new}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+migrate -\/ Transfer the memcg data from the old to the new folio. @old:\+ Currently circulating folio. @new:\+ Replacement folio.

Transfer the memcg data from the old folio to the new folio for migration. The old folio\textquotesingle{}s data info will be cleared. Note that the memory counters will remain unchanged throughout the process.

Both folios must be locked, @new-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}mapping must be set up.

\Hypertarget{memcontrol_8c_a7bc22f3f28d17ceecb4feb5b2db15d93}\index{memcontrol.c@{memcontrol.c}!mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats@{mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats}}
\index{mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats@{mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats}!memcontrol.c@{memcontrol.c}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats()}{mem\_cgroup\_wb\_stats()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8c_a7bc22f3f28d17ceecb4feb5b2db15d93} 
void mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+wb\+\_\+stats (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct bdi\+\_\+writeback \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{wb}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{pfilepages}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{pheadroom}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{pdirty}{, }\item[{unsigned long \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{pwriteback}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+wb\+\_\+stats -\/ retrieve writeback related stats from its memcg @wb:\+ bdi\+\_\+writeback in question @pfilepages:\+ out parameter for number of file pages @pheadroom:\+ out parameter for number of allocatable pages according to memcg @pdirty:\+ out parameter for number of dirty pages @pwriteback:\+ out parameter for number of pages under writeback

Determine the numbers of file, headroom, dirty, and writeback pages in @wb\textquotesingle{}s memcg. File, dirty and writeback are self-\/explanatory. Headroom is a bit more involved.

A memcg\textquotesingle{}s headroom is "{}min(max, high) -\/ used"{}. In the hierarchy, the headroom is calculated as the lowest headroom of itself and the ancestors. Note that this doesn\textquotesingle{}t consider the actual amount of available memory in the system. The caller should further cap \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}@pheadroom accordingly. 

Definition at line \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8c_source_l03414}{3414}} of file \doxymbox{\hyperlink{memcontrol_8c_source}{memcontrol.\+c}}.

mod\+\_\+lruvec\+\_\+state -\/ update lruvec memory statistics @lruvec:\+ the lruvec @idx:\+ the stat item @val:\+ delta to add to the counter, can be negative

The lruvec is the intersection of the NUMA node and a cgroup. This function updates the all three counters that are affected by a change of state at this level:\+ per-\/node, per-\/cgroup, per-\/lruvec.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{obj\_cgroup\_may\_zswap()}{obj\_cgroup\_may\_zswap()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8c_afb088148a129989b51ab511d22e48dfd} 
bool obj\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+may\+\_\+zswap (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structobj__cgroup}{obj\+\_\+cgroup}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{objcg}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

obj\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+may\+\_\+zswap -\/ check if this cgroup can zswap @objcg:\+ the object cgroup

Check if the hierarchical zswap limit has been reached.

This doesn\textquotesingle{}t check for specific headroom, and it is not atomic either. But with zswap, the size of the allocation is only known once compression has occurred, and this optimistic pre-\/check avoids spending cycles on compression when there is already no room left or zswap is disabled altogether somewhere in the hierarchy.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{memcontrol_8c_a708919eeec0f3dc042c9a5303dcc2048} 
u8 mem\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+events\+\_\+index \+[NR\+\_\+\+VM\+\_\+\+EVENT\+\_\+\+ITEMS]\+ \+\_\+\+\_\+read\+\_\+mostly}

These bitmasks identify subsystems with specific features to avoid having to do iterative checks repeatedly.

Write protected by cgroup\+\_\+mutex and write-\/lock of cgroup\+\_\+threadgroup\+\_\+rwsem, read protected by either.

Can only be turned on, but not turned off.
